  • 那个 标签 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{labels}}{}[l]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 结果类型 \(\epsilon\) 匹配 联合国 标签 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{labels}}{}[l]\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/matching.html#match-resulttype}{\vdash} \epsilon \href{../valid/matching.html#match-resulttype}{\leq} C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{labels}}{}[l] }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-catch}{\vdash} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-control}{\mathsf{catch\_all}}~l : \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-catch}{\mathsf{ok}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-control}{\mathsf{catch\_all\_ref}}~l\)¶

那个 渔获条款 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-control}{\mathsf{catch\_all\_ref}}~l)\) 这是 无效 如果:

  • 那个 标签 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{labels}}{}[l]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 结果类型 \((\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-reftype}{\mathsf{ref}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-heaptype}{\mathsf{exn}})\) 匹配 联合国 标签 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{labels}}{}[l]\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/matching.html#match-resulttype}{\vdash} (\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-reftype}{\mathsf{ref}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-heaptype}{\mathsf{exn}}) \href{../valid/matching.html#match-resulttype}{\leq} C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{labels}}{}[l] }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-catch}{\vdash} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-control}{\mathsf{catch\_all\_ref}}~l : \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-catch}{\mathsf{ok}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-table}{\mathsf{table{.}copy}}~x~y\)¶

那个 指令 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-table}{\mathsf{table{.}copy}}~x_1~x_2)\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{at}}_1~{\mathit{at}}_2~{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-addrtype}{\mathit{addrtype}}}~\rightarrow~\epsilon\) 如果:

  • 那个 图表 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x_1]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 图表 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x_1]\) 形式为 \(({\mathit{at}}_1~{\mathit{lim}}_1~{\mathit{rt}}_1)\).

  • 那个 图表 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x_2]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 图表 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x_2]\) 形式为 \(({\mathit{at}}_2~{\mathit{lim}}_2~{\mathit{rt}}_2)\).

  • 那个 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}}_2\) 匹配 联合国 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}}_1\).

  • 那个 地址 类型 \({\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-addrtype}{\mathit{addrtype}}}\) 这是 \({\href{../syntax/types.html#aux-addrtype-min}{\mathrm{min}}}({\mathit{at}}_1, {\mathit{at}}_2)\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x_1] = {\mathit{at}}_1~{\mathit{lim}}_1~{\mathit{rt}}_1 \qquad C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x_2] = {\mathit{at}}_2~{\mathit{lim}}_2~{\mathit{rt}}_2 \qquad C \href{../valid/matching.html#match-reftype}{\vdash} {\mathit{rt}}_2 \href{../valid/matching.html#match-reftype}{\leq} {\mathit{rt}}_1 }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-table}{\mathsf{table{.}copy}}~x_1~x_2 : {\mathit{at}}_1~{\mathit{at}}_2~{\href{../syntax/types.html#aux-addrtype-min}{\mathrm{min}}}({\mathit{at}}_1, {\mathit{at}}_2) \rightarrow \epsilon } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-table}{\mathsf{table{.}init}}~x~y\)¶

那个 指令 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-table}{\mathsf{table{.}init}}~x~y)\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{at}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}~\rightarrow~\epsilon\) 如果:

  • 那个 图表 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 元素段 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{elems}}{}[y]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 元素段 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{elems}}{}[y]\) 形式为 \({\mathit{rt}}_2\).

  • 那个 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}}_2\) 匹配 联合国 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}}_1\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{tables}}{}[x] = {\mathit{at}}~{\mathit{lim}}~{\mathit{rt}}_1 \qquad C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{elems}}{}[y] = {\mathit{rt}}_2 \qquad C \href{../valid/matching.html#match-reftype}{\vdash} {\mathit{rt}}_2 \href{../valid/matching.html#match-reftype}{\leq} {\mathit{rt}}_1 }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-table}{\mathsf{table{.}init}}~x~y : {\mathit{at}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} \rightarrow \epsilon } \qquad \end{array}\]

内存指令¶

内存指令 使用情况 内存参数, (中文). 分类 地址 类型 页:1 和 位宽 他们适合的进入。

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}\)¶

\(\{ \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{align}}~n,\;\allowbreak \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{offset}}~m \}\) 对 \({\mathit{at}}\) 和 \(N\) 如果:

  • \({2^{n}}\) 小于或等于 \(N / 8\).

  • \(m\) 低于 \({2^{{|{\mathit{at}}|}}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ {2^{n}} \leq N / 8 \qquad m < {2^{{|{\mathit{at}}|}}} }{ {\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-memarg}{\vdash}}\, \{ \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{align}}~n,\;\allowbreak \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{offset}}~m \} : {\mathit{at}} \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-memarg}{\rightarrow} N } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{load}}~x~\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{load}}~x~{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{at}}~\rightarrow~{\mathit{nt}}\) 如果:

  • 那个 内存 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{mems}}{}[x]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 内存 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{mems}}{}[x]\) 形式为 \(({\mathit{at}}~{\mathit{lim}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-memtype}{\mathsf{page}})\).

  • \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}}\) 对 \({\mathit{at}}\) 和 \({|{\mathit{nt}}|}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{mems}}{}[x] = {\mathit{at}}~{\mathit{lim}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-memtype}{\mathsf{page}} \qquad {\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-memarg}{\vdash}}\, {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}} : {\mathit{at}} \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-memarg}{\rightarrow} {|{\mathit{nt}}|} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{nt}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{load}}~x~{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}} : {\mathit{at}} \rightarrow {\mathit{nt}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{load}}{N}\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}~x~\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{load}}}{{K}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}}}}~x~{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{at}}~\rightarrow~{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}}\) 如果:

  • 那个 内存 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{mems}}{}[x]\) 已存在。

  • 那个 内存 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{mems}}{}[x]\) 形式为 \(({\mathit{at}}~{\mathit{lim}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-memtype}{\mathsf{page}})\).

  • \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}}\) 对 \({\mathit{at}}\) 和 \(K\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{mems}}{}[x] = {\mathit{at}}~{\mathit{lim}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-memtype}{\mathsf{page}} \qquad {\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-memarg}{\vdash}}\, {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}} : {\mathit{at}} \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-memarg}{\rightarrow} K }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{load}}}{{K}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}}}}~x~{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}} : {\mathit{at}} \rightarrow {\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-memory}{\mathsf{store}}{N}~x~\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-memarg}{\mathit{memarg}}\)¶

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-ref}{\mathsf{ref{.}test}}~\mathit{rt}\)¶

那个 指令 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-ref}{\mathsf{ref{.}test}}~{\mathit{rt}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{rt}'}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\) 如果:

  • 那个 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}}\) 这是 无效.

  • 那个 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}'}\) 这是 无效.

  • 那个 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}}\) 匹配 联合国 引用类型 \({\mathit{rt}'}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/types.html#valid-reftype}{\vdash} {\mathit{rt}} : \href{../valid/types.html#valid-reftype}{\mathsf{ok}} \qquad C \href{../valid/types.html#valid-reftype}{\vdash} {\mathit{rt}'} : \href{../valid/types.html#valid-reftype}{\mathsf{ok}} \qquad C \href{../valid/matching.html#match-reftype}{\vdash} {\mathit{rt}} \href{../valid/matching.html#match-reftype}{\leq} {\mathit{rt}'} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-ref}{\mathsf{ref{.}test}}~{\mathit{rt}} : {\mathit{rt}'} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

说明

选择超级型的自由 \({\mathit{rt}'}\) 允许键入 指令 最不准确的超级类型 \({\mathit{rt}}\) 作为输入,即对应堆积中的顶端类型 子类型关系 等级。

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c_{\mathit{nt}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\epsilon~\rightarrow~{\mathit{nt}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{nt}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c_{\mathit{nt}} : \epsilon \rightarrow {\mathit{nt}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-unop}{\mathit{unop}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-unop}{\mathit{unop}}}_{\mathit{nt}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{nt}}~\rightarrow~{\mathit{nt}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-unop}{\mathit{unop}}}_{\mathit{nt}} : {\mathit{nt}} \rightarrow {\mathit{nt}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-binop}{\mathit{binop}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-binop}{\mathit{binop}}}_{\mathit{nt}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{nt}}~{\mathit{nt}}~\rightarrow~{\mathit{nt}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-binop}{\mathit{binop}}}_{\mathit{nt}} : {\mathit{nt}}~{\mathit{nt}} \rightarrow {\mathit{nt}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-testop}{\mathit{testop}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-testop}{\mathit{testop}}}_{\mathit{nt}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{nt}}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-testop}{\mathit{testop}}}_{\mathit{nt}} : {\mathit{nt}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-relop}{\mathit{relop}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-relop}{\mathit{relop}}}_{\mathit{nt}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{nt}}~{\mathit{nt}}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{nt}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-relop}{\mathit{relop}}}_{\mathit{nt}} : {\mathit{nt}}~{\mathit{nt}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(t_1\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-cvtop}{\mathit{cvtop}}\mathsf{\_}t_2\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}^?\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{nt}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-cvtop}{\mathit{cvtop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{nt}}_2})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{nt}}_2~\rightarrow~{\mathit{nt}}_1\).

向量指令¶

向量指令 可以有一个前缀来描述 形状 的操作。 包装数字 类型, \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-storagetype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle8}}\) 和 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-storagetype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle16}}\)没有 价值 类型。辅助函数映射此类组合类型形状以进行值 类型:

\[\begin{split}\begin{array}[t]{@{}lcl@{}l@{}} {\href{../syntax/types.html#aux-unpack}{\mathrm{unpack}}}({{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}}}{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathsf{x}}}{M}) & = & {\href{../syntax/types.html#aux-unpack}{\mathrm{unpack}}}({\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}}) \\ \end{array}\end{split}\]

\(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-vec}{\mathsf{const}}~c\)¶

那个 指令 \((\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c)\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\epsilon~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c : \epsilon \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\mathsf{i8x16.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-vec}{\mathsf{shuffle}}~\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-laneidx}{\mathit{laneidx}}^{16}\)¶

  • 为所有人 \(i\) 输入 \({i^\ast}\):

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ (i < 2 \cdot {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#aux-dim}{\mathrm{dim}}}({\mathit{sh}}))^\ast }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{sh}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-vec}{\mathsf{shuffle}}~{i^\ast} : \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{ishape}}_1\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vextbinop}{\mathit{vextbinop}}\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{ishape}}_2\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{sh}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vextbinop}{\mathit{vextbinop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{sh}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vextbinop}{\mathit{vextbinop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2} : \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{ishape}}_1\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vextternop}{\mathit{vextternop}}\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{ishape}}_2\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{sh}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vextternop}{\mathit{vextternop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{sh}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vextternop}{\mathit{vextternop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2} : \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{ishape}}_1\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-vec}{\mathsf{narrow}}\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{ishape}}_2\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}\)¶

那个 指令 \(({{\mathit{sh}}_1{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-vec}{\mathsf{narrow}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}}})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {{\mathit{sh}}_1{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-vec}{\mathsf{narrow}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}}} : \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{shape}}\mathsf{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vcvtop}{\mathit{vcvtop}}\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-half}{\mathit{half}}^?\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-shape}{\mathit{shape}}\mathsf{\_}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-sx}{\mathit{sx}}^?\mathsf{\_zero}^?\)¶

那个 指令 \(({\mathit{sh}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vcvtop}{\mathit{vcvtop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2})\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}~\rightarrow~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\mathit{sh}}_1 {.} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-vcvtop}{\mathit{vcvtop}}}}{\mathsf{\_}}{{\mathit{sh}}_2} : \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-vectype}{\mathsf{v\scriptstyle128}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

指令 顺序¶

输入 指令 序列是递归定义的。

那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\) 如果:

  • 要么:

    • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 为空。

    • 那个 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\) 形式为 \(\epsilon~\rightarrow~\epsilon\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 形式为 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}'}\).

    • 那个 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\) 形式为 \({t_1^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x^\ast}}\,{t_2^\ast}\).

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}'}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({t_1^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x^\ast}}\,{t_2^\ast}\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 形式为 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_1^\ast}~{{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_2^\ast}\).

    • 那个 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\) 形式为 \({t_1^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x_1^\ast}~{x_2^\ast}}\,{t_3^\ast}\).

    • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_1^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({t_1^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x_1^\ast}}\,{t_2^\ast}\).

    • 为所有人 \(x_1\) 输入 \({x_1^\ast}\):

      • 那个 当地 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{locals}}{}[x_1]\) 已存在。

      • 那个 当地 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{locals}}{}[x_1]\) 形式为 \(({\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-init}{\mathit{init}}}~t)\).

    • 在背景之下 \(C\) 与当地 类型 页:1 \({x_1^\ast}\) 更新到 \({(\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-init}{\mathsf{set}}~t)^\ast}\),则 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_2^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({t_2^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x_2^\ast}}\,{t_3^\ast}\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}''}\).

    • 那个 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}''}\) 匹配 联合国 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\).

    • 那个 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\) 这是 无效.

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 类型 \({\mathit{it}}\) 形式为 \({t^\ast}~{t_1^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x^\ast}}\,{t^\ast}~{t_2^\ast}\).

    • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \({t_1^\ast}~{\mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}}_{{x^\ast}}\,{t_2^\ast}\).

    • 那个 结果类型 \({t^\ast}\) 这是 无效.

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} \epsilon : \epsilon \rightarrow \epsilon } \qquad \end{array}\]
\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instr}{\vdash} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}} : {t_1^\ast} \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x^\ast}} {t_2^\ast} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}} : {t_1^\ast} \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x^\ast}} {t_2^\ast} } \qquad \end{array}\]
\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_1^\ast} : {t_1^\ast} \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x_1^\ast}} {t_2^\ast} \qquad (C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{locals}}{}[x_1] = {\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-init}{\mathit{init}}}~t)^\ast \qquad C{}[{.}\href{../syntax/modules.html#syntax-local}{\mathsf{local}}{}[{x_1^\ast}] = {(\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-init}{\mathsf{set}}~t)^\ast}] \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_2^\ast} : {t_2^\ast} \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x_2^\ast}} {t_3^\ast} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_1^\ast}~{{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}_2^\ast} : {t_1^\ast} \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x_1^\ast}~{x_2^\ast}} {t_3^\ast} } \qquad \end{array}\]
\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast} : {\mathit{it}} \qquad C \href{../valid/matching.html#match-instrtype}{\vdash} {\mathit{it}} \href{../valid/matching.html#match-instrtype}{\leq} {\mathit{it}'} \qquad C \href{../valid/types.html#valid-instrtype}{\vdash} {\mathit{it}'} : \href{../valid/types.html#valid-instrtype}{\mathsf{ok}} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast} : {\mathit{it}'} } \qquad \end{array}\]

说明

这个 子假设规则 允许削弱类型 指令 序列为超型, 包括降低变量的能力 \({x^\ast}\) 从 指令 类型,在不需要时,例如,在正文末尾。 块.

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast} : {t_1^\ast} \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x^\ast}} {t_2^\ast} \qquad C \href{../valid/types.html#valid-resulttype}{\vdash} {t^\ast} : \href{../valid/types.html#valid-resulttype}{\mathsf{ok}} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast} : ({t^\ast}~{t_1^\ast}) \mathrel{\href{../valid/conventions.html#syntax-instrtype}{\rightarrow}}_{{x^\ast}} ({t^\ast}~{t_2^\ast}) } \qquad \end{array}\]

说明

结合前两条规则, 这个 栈帧 规则 允许作曲 指令 谁 类型 否则将不直接适用。 例如,考虑 指令 顺序

\[(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~1)~(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~2)~(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} {.} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{add}})\]

要输入此序列, 它的子序列 \((\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~2)~(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} {.} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{add}})\) 需要以中间类型有效。 但直接类型的 \((\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~2)\) 这是 \(\epsilon \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\)没有 匹配 (a) 预期得到的2项投入 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} {.} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{add}}\)。 。 。 规则允许削弱 \((\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~2)\) 到超级类型 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\),从而可以由 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} {.} \href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{add}}\) 生成中间类型 \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}} \rightarrow \href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}~\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\) 为子序. 这又可以由第一个常数组成。

表达式¶

表达式 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-expr}{\mathit{expr}}}\) 按分类 结果 类型 \({t^\ast}\).

那个 表达式 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 结果类型 \({t^\ast}\) 如果:

  • 那个 指令 顺序 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 这是 无效 与 指令 类型 \(\epsilon~\rightarrow~{t^\ast}\).

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-instrs}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast} : \epsilon \rightarrow {t^\ast} }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-expr}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast} : {t^\ast} } \qquad \end{array}\]

常数表达式¶

在一个 常数 表达式,全部 指令 必须是恒定的。

\({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\) 常数,如果:

  • 为所有人 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 输入 \({{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}\):

    • \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 是常数。

\({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 常数,如果:

  • 要么:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \(({\mathit{nt}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c_{\mathit{nt}})\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \(({\mathit{vt}}{.}\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{const}}~c_{\mathit{vt}})\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-ref}{\mathsf{ref{.}null}}~{\mathit{ht}})\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-i31}{\mathsf{ref{.}i\scriptstyle31}}\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-ref}{\mathsf{ref{.}func}}~x)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-struct}{\mathsf{struct{.}new}}~x)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-struct}{\mathsf{struct{.}new\_default}}~x)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-array}{\mathsf{array{.}new}}~x)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-array}{\mathsf{array{.}new\_default}}~x)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-array}{\mathsf{array{.}new\_fixed}}~x~n)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-extern}{\mathsf{any{.}convert\_extern}}\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-extern}{\mathsf{extern{.}convert\_any}}\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \((\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-variable}{\mathsf{global{.}get}}~x)\).

    • 那个 全球 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{globals}}{}[x]\) 已存在。

    • 那个 全球 \(C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{globals}}{}[x]\) 形式为 \((\epsilon~t)\).

  • 或曰:

    • 那个 指令 \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}\) 形式为 \(({\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}} {.} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-binop}{\mathit{binop}}})\).

    • \({\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}}\) 载于[\(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle32}}\); \(\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i\scriptstyle64}}\)].

    • \({\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-binop}{\mathit{binop}}}\) 载于[\(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{add}}\); \(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{sub}}\); \(\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-numeric}{\mathsf{mul}}\)].

\[\begin{array}{@{}c@{}}\displaystyle \frac{ (C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\vdash} {\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}~\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\mathsf{const}})^\ast }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\vdash} {{\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr}{\mathit{instr}}}^\ast}~\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\mathsf{const}} } \qquad \end{array}\]

}{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\vdash} ({\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\mathsf{i}}}{{\href{../syntax/types.html#syntax-numtype}{\scriptstyle\kern-0.1emN}}} {.} (\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-array}{\mathsf{array{.}new\_fixed}}~x~n)~\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\mathsf{const}} } \\[3ex]\displaystyle \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\vdash} (\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-extern}{\mathsf{any{.}convert\_extern}})~\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\mathsf{const}} } \qquad \frac{ }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\vdash} (\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-extern}{\mathsf{extern{.}convert\_any}})~\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\mathsf{const}} } \\[3ex]\displaystyle \frac{ C{.}\href{../valid/conventions.html#context}{\mathsf{globals}}{}[x] = t }{ C \href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\vdash} (\href{../syntax/instructions.html#syntax-instr-variable}{\mathsf{global{.}get}}~x)~\href{../valid/instructions.html#valid-constant}{\mathsf{const}} } \qquad \end{array}\end{split}\]

说明

目前,经常表达式出现在 全球 被进一步限制在其中 \(\mathsf{global{.}get}\) 指令 仅允许提及 进口 或 先前定义 环球网. 发生于 图表 也许只有 \(\mathsf{global{.}get}\) 指令 提及 进口 环球网. 这一点在 验证 规则 模块 限制上下文 \(C\) 相应.

定义 常量表达式 可在以后的版本中延长 WebAssembly.
